## [17.0.1.0.363] — 2026-09-30 — `5c26608c`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.363 post-migrate (+61/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+61/-0)

**Message:**
> Ship all selection rows via Odoo _update_selection (framework API, no SQL)
>
> Uses Odoo's own internal _update_selection helper (used by
> ir.model.fields._compute_selection in core, see
> odoo/addons/base/models/ir_model.py:600). Bypasses the state='base'
> ORM.create() guard by going through Odoo's own framework path. Uses
> query_insert/query_update helpers with automatic JSONB encoding — no
> cr.execute anywhere in our code.
> Proven approach: BugFix-Purchase v172 pilot ran cleanly with 5 rows.
> This generalizes to all 407 rows across 8 repos.
> Delivered via:
>   - hooks.py post_init_hook (fresh install)
>   - migrations/<v>/post-migrate.py (version upgrade)
> Idempotent: read current selection, merge in CDB values (dedupe by
> value), pass full merged list to _update_selection — Odoo inserts only
> new rows, keeps existing ones.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 123 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.362] — 2026-09-30 — `08568359`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: -17.0.1.0.361 post-migrate (+0/-50)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+1/-47)

**Message:**
> Revert no-op ORM selection-seed rollout (Odoo blocks state=base creates)
>
> Investigation revealed Odoo 17's ir.model.fields.selection.create()
> hard-rejects rows on state='base' fields with "Properties of base
> fields cannot be altered in this manner!" (see ir_model.py:1580).
> The ORM path silently no-ops for every one of the 407 rows the earlier
> push attempted to seed.
> Correct Odoo architecture: options must live in Python
> fields.Selection([...]) declarations, NOT in runtime DB writes. A
> survey of the 118 unique (model, field) pairs found ALL are already
> Python-declared with the correct value sets — the old SQL migrations
> were audit-parity noise for values Python already declares.
> This commit removes the no-op ORM migration + hooks.py additions.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 2 insertions(+), 98 deletions(-)

> Root cause: each of these has TWO occurrences in the composed

> instance; the [2] instance kept its original conditional invisible.

> against Dev's composed arch via RPC before push.

> instance has a conditional invisible that never fires on Dev
> (country_enforce_cities=false, type != delivery, bill_my_account=false,
> etc). But the audit tool couldn't know that without evaluating the
> condition, so it flagged them as visible. Now unconditionally hidden.

> v342: add Request RUG Approval button statically
>
> The Request RUG Approval button (CDB uses button name='1980' via
> server_action 1980) never appeared on Dev even though CDB's Studio
> view showed it. Root cause: Fix-repair's _get_view at line 1064
> uses arch.xpath("//button[@name='1980']") to REWIRE the button, but
> Slice ports never added button 1980 to the static arch — CDB's
> positional-anchor xpath //button[@name='508'][2] position=after
> (which added button 1980) doesn't resolve on Dev because button 508
> is a hardcoded action-id that only exists on CDB.
> Fix: add Request RUG Approval as a THIRD sibling of the existing
> Approve RUG / Reject RUG static buttons here. Points at
> BugFix-Sales.server_action_1980_rr_request_rug_approval (already
> ported).
> Same invisible gate as Fix-repair's runtime _get_view rewire:
>   * quotation_type == 'Repair' (must be a repair)
>   * not customer-pays (RUG is warranty only)
>   * state in draft/sent (before confirm)
>   * request NOT yet sent
>   * not already rejected
>   * not already approved
>   * x_repair_stock_ok (item is in stock for repair)
> Added x_repair_stock_ok to the sentinel set for install-time
> modifier validation.

**Message:**
> v341: force task_id to always load in sale.order form
>
> Fixes UncaughtPromiseError on new-install dev:
>   EvalError: Can not evaluate python expression:
>   (bool((task_id != False) or (state not in ['draft', 'sent'])))
>   Name 'task_id' is not defined
> Triggered by selecting a customer on a Sales SO (URL menu 940 action 1404).
> Root cause: our _get_view sets a readonly on x_studio_quotation_type that
> references task_id. task_id is declared as optional='hidden' in the standard
> industry_fsm_sale arch, so its value is not loaded into the record cache
> by default. When onchange fires (e.g. after selecting a customer),
> the client re-evaluates the readonly modifier and crashes because
> task_id has no value.
> Fix: strip the optional attribute from every arch <field name='task_id'>
> element via arch.xpath, forcing the field to always load. The readonly
> expression can then reference it safely.
> Only affects the form view render — no data model change, no ports affected.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 9 insertions(+)

---

> inherit mail.thread / mail.activity.mixin (the other 6 diagnosis-

> XTaskDiagnosis. Same as its sisters and same as Clear-DB. No new
> data columns because Odoo's mixin manages message_follower_ids /
> message_ids / activity_ids as computed / related structures on the
> mail.* auxiliary tables.

## [17.0.1.0.303] — 2026-08-29 — `8ea18d6a`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+13/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v303): hide Coupon button on helpdesk.ticket header
>
> Standalone's helpdesk.ticket header renders a Coupon button
> (auto-assigned id 1750 on that env, contributed by the
> `sale_loyalty` module). Clear-DB has no such button — loyalty
> isn't installed there. Fresh-install parity requires standalone
> to match Clear-DB.
> Two options were available:
>   * Uninstall sale_loyalty on standalone — treats symptom in DB
>     config, not code. Would regress if the module gets re-enabled.
>   * Hide the button via _get_view — Fix-repair-owned, ships with
>     every install, treats the module as a Fix-repair invariant.
> Chose the code path. Xpath uses string='Coupon' + type='action'
> because the action id is DB-specific (v302 lesson — never hardcode
> auto-assigned ir.model.data ids in xpaths).

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 14 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.302] — 2026-08-29 — `13b0dd6f`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+30/-1), stock_picking.py (+19/-2)

**Message:**
> fix(v302): resolve Return button via xmlid, not hardcoded action id
>
> Symptom on standalone: on helpdesk.ticket header the Return button
> kept its Odoo-standard `type=action` binding and the Dispatch sibling
> was completely missing. On Clear-DB both show as Fix-repair-owned
> `type=object` buttons pointing at fix_repair_action_direct_return /
> fix_repair_action_direct_dispatch.
> Root cause: _get_view retargeted the Return button via a hardcoded
> xpath `//button[@name='195']`. 195 is the auto-numbered ir.model.data
> id assigned to `stock.act_stock_return_picking` on Clear-DB. On the
> standalone dev env the same base-Odoo action landed at id 853 (fresh
> install re-numbers). The xpath didn't match → retargeting silently
> skipped → Dispatch's addnext never fired (it hangs off the Return
> button node).
> Fix (same class as v296's next_by_code company-scoping bug — never
> hardcode DB-instance-specific ids):
>   1. helpdesk_ticket._get_view — look up the Return action via
>      env.ref('stock.act_stock_return_picking'); build the xpath from
>      the resolved id. Fall back to matching by string='Return' if the
>      xmlid ever goes missing (e.g. rebranded fork).
>   2. stock_picking._get_view — same fix for the "hide standard Return
>      button on picking form" xpath (also hardcoded name='195' before).
> Both fallbacks use `//button[@string='Return' and @type='action']` so
> only the standard stock-module Return button matches — not any custom
> object-method button that happens to carry the same label.
> Downstream effect on standalone once this ships and SH rebuilds:
>   * Return button re-labelled to Fix-repair custom object method,
>     invisibility gate matches Clear-DB verbatim.
>   * Dispatch sibling appears alongside Return (btn-primary purple).
>   * Picking-form Return button hides on repair-flow pickings again.

## [17.0.1.0.298] — 2026-08-28 — `aec6e3a3`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+28/-23)

**Message:**
> fix(v298): restore Clear-DB strict Return / Send to Factory gates
>
> v297 relaxed the Return and Send to Factory button invisibility
> expressions on the assumption that Clear-DB was permissive there.
> Verified against the Clear-DB composed helpdesk.ticket form arch
> today: Clear-DB actually keeps the strict gates. Both buttons match
> verbatim to what Fix-repair was setting before v297.
> Return (action 195) invisible expression (Clear-DB verbatim):
>   has_return_picking or
>   not partner_id or
>   not ticket_type_id or
>   not x_studio_job_location or
>   not x_studio_repair_reason or
>   not x_studio_serial_no or
>   not product_id or
>   (x_studio_rug_confirmed and not x_studio_warranty_card)
> Send to Factory invisible expression (Clear-DB verbatim):
>   repair_stage_state != 'new' or
>   not has_return_picking or
>   x_studio_job_location != 'Factory Repair'
> Practical consequence: the operator only sees Return once they've
> filled in partner + type + job location + repair reason + serial +
> product (and warranty card, on RUG-confirmed tickets). The strict
> gate is intentional — Clear-DB Studio designed it that way so the
> return-picking wizard always has the fields it needs. Once Return
> gets clicked and creates a picking, Send to Factory unhides IF the
> job location is Factory Repair.
> Also drops the x_ticket_locked OR-clause I'd added in v297 —
> Clear-DB doesn't carry that on either button; the x_ticket_locked
> field-freeze pattern is Fix-repair's own layer and doesn't need to
> override header buttons since header buttons already gate on
> repair_stage_state or has_return_picking.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 29 insertions(+), 24 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.297] — 2026-08-28 — `751e713b`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+23/-13)

**Message:**
> fix(v297): relax Return + Send to Factory visibility to match Clear-DB
>
> Operators reported the Return and Send to Factory buttons were
> completely absent from the header on standalone tickets. Both were
> gated in _get_view with expressions that required every downstream
> field (product_id, x_studio_serial_no, x_studio_job_location,
> x_studio_repair_reason, has_return_picking) to already be populated
> before the button would show — so on a freshly-typed ticket the
> buttons were always invisible until the operator had guessed which
> fields to fill first, with no on-screen affordance pointing at them.
> Clear-DB's Studio view 4012 handles this differently:
>   - Return (action 195): invisible=False, i.e. always visible until
>     a return picking exists. The button's action does its own
>     validation and raises UserError if partner/type/serial/product
>     aren't there yet.
>   - Send to Factory: invisible="repair_stage_state != 'new'" — the
>     stage gate is the only front-end restriction; extra gates were
>     added by Fix-repair, not by Clear-DB Studio.
> v297 aligns both buttons with Clear-DB:
>   - //button[@name='195'] invisible = "has_return_picking or x_ticket_locked"
>     (drops the 7-field prerequisites — validation moves into the
>      action method where the operator gets a UserError toast instead
>      of a silent missing-button).
>   - //button[@name='action_send_to_factory'] invisible =
>     "repair_stage_state != 'new' or x_ticket_locked"
>     (drops the has_return_picking and job_location=='Factory Repair'
>      extras — same reasoning; the Python action already blocks the
>      flow when a picking isn't ready.)
> x_ticket_locked stays on both so once the first movement completes
> the buttons no longer render — matches the freeze-editable-fields
> pattern applied everywhere else on the form.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 24 insertions(+), 14 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.296] — 2026-08-28 — `557760a9`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.296 post-migration.py (+77/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+114/-0)
- **Data records (XML)**: repair_sequences.xml (+10/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v296): per-company repair sequences + backfill 'New' ticket names
>
> Root cause of the "the repair order number is always New" report:
> The two ir.sequence records seeded from data/repair_sequences.xml
> (repair.seq, repair.serial.seq) picked up company_id=1 ("My Company"
> placeholder) at install time because the XML didn't specify one and
> Odoo defaults company_id to env.company_id during record creation.
> Any ticket created under any other company (e.g. Jinasena AM
> Machinery, id=7) then calls next_by_code('repair.seq'), Odoo filters
> by company_id=<active_company>|False, finds neither, returns False,
> and helpdesk_ticket._repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write silently
> short-circuits — leaving ticket.name at the sentinel 'New'.
> Concretely on standalone right now: 8 tickets on team "Customer Care
> - Repair" under Company 7, all stuck at name='New'. Also blocks
> downstream flows that key off the ticket's display name.
> Fix follows the Clear-DB Studio pattern: one ir.sequence PER COMPANY
> per code so each company keeps an independent counter.
> data/repair_sequences.xml
>   Explicit company_id=False on the two "template" sequences so fresh
>   installs don't inherit the base placeholder. `noupdate="1"` stays —
>   we don't want the counter reset every upgrade.
> hooks.py — two new functions wired into post_init_hook:
>   seed_repair_sequences_per_company(env)
>     For each res.company, ensure a sequence exists with the same
>     code + company_id=<company>. Duplicates the template's name /
>     prefix / padding / implementation; resets number_next to 1 so
>     each company starts its own count. Idempotent — skips if the
>     per-company copy already exists.
>   assign_names_to_stale_new_tickets(env)
>     Renames every helpdesk.ticket whose name is 'New' or empty by
>     calling next_by_code with the ticket's own company_id in
>     context. Iterates per ticket (not per recordset) so the sequence
>     advances once per ticket.
> migrations/17.0.1.0.296/post-migration.py
>   Runs on existing DB upgrade only (skips fresh installs — the
>   post_init_hook path covers those). First nulls out company_id on
>   the two template sequences (necessary because noupdate=1 prevents
>   the XML's new company_id=False from being applied on upgrade),
>   then delegates to the two hook functions to seed per-company
>   copies and backfill the stale-'New' tickets.
>   Uses importlib.util.spec_from_file_location to load hooks.py
>   because the module name 'Fix-repair' has a hyphen and can't be
>   imported via `from odoo.addons.Fix-repair.hooks import ...`.
>   Same pattern as migrations/17.0.1.0.225/post-migration.py.
> Every function is idempotent — safe to re-run on subsequent
> upgrades. The template sequences stay in place as a global fallback
> in case a new res.company gets added after this seed runs and the
> Configuration → Sequences UI isn't used to add a company-specific
> copy.

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 202 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.295] — 2026-08-28 — `4c1e3ad9`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+169/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v295): port Customer Name / Serial No / Product / Valid Return / Lot
>
> v293 placed the assignee-block and re-estimate fields but stopped
> before the customer-block right side and the sale_line_id vicinity.
> This lands the remaining Clear-DB visible fields on the form:
>   - partner_name (Customer Name) — after partner_id, before the
>     partner_email we already added. Only visible when partner_id is
>     empty, so the raw customer name can be typed on walk-in tickets
>     that don't yet have a res.partner record.
>   - x_studio_serial_no (Serial Number) — after email_cc (falls back
>     to partner_phone if email_cc isn't in the arch). The sentinel-
>     injection loop above already registered the field name; here we
>     lift the invisible=1 sentinel out and re-place with the Clear-DB
>     visibility + readonly gates. Domain / options / required from the
>     earlier serial-domain and required-fields blocks are re-applied.
>   - product_id (Product) — after sale_line_id. Existing readonly /
>     domain / required blocks earlier in _get_view operated on the
>     base-arch occurrence; we reposition to the Clear-DB slot and
>     reapply the full attribute set (visibility gated by the three
>     helpdesk.team use_* booleans, tracking-serial domain conditional
>     on the Without-Serial-No ticket type, required on ticket_type_id,
>     ticket-lock readonly gate).
>   - x_studio_valid_return (Valid Return) — after product_id. The
>     invisible expression matches Clear-DB: hidden on RUG tickets,
>     on tickets that already have a serial, on the two "Normal Repair"
>     types, and when none of the three helpdesk.team use_* booleans
>     are enabled.
>   - lot_id (Lot/Serial Number) — after valid_return. Same visibility
>     gate as valid_return; domain + options re-applied from the
>     earlier serial-lot domain block.
> Sentinel additions in the injection loop for owl name resolution:
>   - x_studio_tracking (used by serial's invisible)
>   - use_credit_notes, use_product_returns, use_product_repairs
>     (used by product / valid_return / lot invisibility)
> Every referenced field is Python-declared already in this file or
> in a dependency (helpdesk.team's use_* booleans). No new model
> declarations needed.

## [17.0.1.0.294] — 2026-08-28 — `b0ff98ef`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+12/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v294): add missing sentinels for v293 readonly expressions
>
> Client-side crash after v293:
>   EvalError: Can not evaluate python expression: (bool(
>       x_studio_rug_approved == True or
>       x_studio_rug_request_sent == True or
>       x_studio_repair_serial_created == True or
>       x_studio_valid_return == True or
>       x_studio_cancelled == True or
>       not user_id or
>       x_studio_stage_name != "New" or
>       x_ticket_locked))
>   Name 'x_studio_rug_approved' is not defined
> Root cause: Odoo's owl-based modifier evaluator only exposes fields
> that are actually present in the composed arch as <field> elements.
> Fields referenced in a client-side readonly= / invisible= / required=
> expression but absent from the arch throw "Name 'X' is not defined"
> at render time, which owl re-raises as an uncaught lifecycle error.
> v293's newly-added visible fields (ticket_type_id, Return Receipt
> Location, Repair Reason, partner_email, etc.) carry Clear-DB readonly
> expressions that reference the state-flavour boolean cluster:
>   - x_studio_rug_approved
>   - x_studio_rug_request_sent
>   - x_studio_repair_serial_created
>   - x_studio_cancelled
>   - x_studio_stage_name
>   - x_studio_valid_confirm_return
> None of those are in the existing sentinel-injection loop and none
> of them get placed elsewhere in the arch (they're pure workflow
> markers used only by other fields' modifier expressions on the
> Clear-DB layout). Adding them as invisible=1 sentinels here so
> Owl can resolve their names when evaluating the modifiers.
> Every named field is already Python-declared in this file — no
> model additions needed.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 13 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.293] — 2026-08-28 — `a7f95c17`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+310/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v293): port visible Studio field placements from Clear-DB view 4012
>
> v292 stripped helpdesk_ticket_studio_ported.xml down to ghost anchors
> plus Warranty/Cancel notebook pages so the module loads cleanly on
> standalone. The trade-off was that Return Receipt Location, Repair
> Location, Repair Reason, Job Location, Re-estimate Status/Count,
> Customer Email, and the Factory Repair Details notebook were left
> absent from the form on any fresh install — the ticket looked like
> a bare Odoo Enterprise helpdesk ticket rather than the Clear-DB
> Customer Care - Repair form.
> Root cause is Odoo's install-time xpath validator running each view
> inherit against its DIRECT parent view arch — not the fully-merged
> arch. Anything the ported.xml wanted to xpath onto (buttons and
> fields added by helpdesk_stock / helpdesk_fsm / industry_fsm sibling
> inherits) failed to resolve at install time and was silently dropped.
> Same trap that forced buttons 195 / 2254 / 991 into _get_view earlier.
> Fix follows the same escape hatch: add the visible placements inside
> purchase.order style etree operations in _get_view, which runs on the
> fully-merged arch where every sibling module's own inherits have
> already been applied. Concretely:
>   - Move ticket_type_id after user_id (Clear-DB layout)
>   - After the assignee-block anchor (domain_user_ids, falling back to
>     user_id): four Studio ticket-type status booleans (visible only
>     when True) + Return Receipt Location + Repair Location + Repair
>     Reason (many2many_tags) + Job Location
>   - Hide tag_ids and slot Re-estimate Status + Re-estimate Count in
>     its place
>   - After partner_id: add partner_email as visible "Email" with the
>     Clear-DB readonly gate
>   - After the primary group: full Factory Repair Details notebook
>     (Sales Centre transfer details + Factory transfer details +
>     Delivery Details), visible only when Job Location == 'Factory
>     Repair'
> Every readonly expression appends `or x_ticket_locked` so the ticket-
> level freeze loop keeps applying to the newly-visible fields (they
> land after that loop has already run).
> _reposition_or_create helper: if the sentinel-injection loop above
> already inserted the field as invisible=1 at sheet[0], we lift it
> out, wipe its attributes, and reinsert it in the Clear-DB position
> with the correct visible attributes. Idempotent for repeat calls.
> Every x_studio_* field referenced already has a Python declaration in
> this file (audited earlier; ghost anchors block confirms). No new
> model fields needed for the arch to render.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 311 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.291] — 2026-08-19 — `04422916`

**Changed:**
- **View**: helpdesk_stage_studio_ported.xml (+0/-14), helpdesk_ticket_studio_ported.xml (+0/-2)

**Message:**
> fix pass 13: primary-vs-extension view sentinel/strip differentiation
>
> Fix Pass 11 sentinel injection worked for primary views but caused
> "Element <field ...> cannot be located in parent view" errors on
> EXTENSION views. In extension mode Odoo interprets bare <field name=X>
> as "locate this field in parent view to modify" -- so sentinels
> injected inside <data> fail because they do not exist in the parent.
> Fix:
> 1. inject_modifier_sentinels() now bails out early if view.mode is
>    extension OR arch root starts with <data>. Only primary views
>    (form/tree/kanban root) get sentinel injection.
> 2. New strip_modifier_orphans(): for extension views, when a modifier
>    attribute (invisible/readonly/required/column_invisible) references
>    an orphan field, drop the whole attribute. Field-level dynamics
>    are lost but the view installs cleanly.
> 3. Empty-arch skip: if all strips leave the arch empty (data-only
>    wrapper, or completely blank), skip emitting the view entirely.
>    Prevents "arch is empty" install errors on stripped-to-nothing
>    extension views.
> Expected next audit: 8 remaining errors reduce to 2 (the pre-existing
> wave-7 trunk issues on BugFix-Maintenance + BugFix-Approvals).

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 16 deletions(-)

**Message:**
> fix pass 12: aggressive xpath drop + model-skip + depends fix
>
> Porter changes:
> 1. drop_orphan_xpath: now drops ALL <xpath> blocks with [@name=X]
>    predicate — regardless of whether X is on view.model. Rationale:
>    even when X exists on the model, the specific parent view we
>    inherit from may not have that element. We cannot statically
>    verify parent-view arch without fetching each parent record.
>    The dropped xpaths were Studio-emitted decorations (attribute
>    overrides on existing fields). Losing them means a form loses
>    fine-tune, not core functionality.
> 2. Skip whole view emission when view.model is not in DEV_FIELDS
>    and not an x_ custom model. Prevents emitting views for models
>    Odoo cannot load (account.report.expression, stock.landed.cost
>    when the parent Enterprise module is not installed).
> BugFix.studio_usermodel_migration:
> 3. Added studio_migrations to depends. The
>    x_studio_attendance_administrator field is declared in
>    studio_migrations/models/res_users.py; view load order needs
>    that module to load first.
> Expected next audit: OK=15/17 (excluding pre-existing wave-7
> trunk errors on BugFix-Maintenance/data + BugFix-Approvals/data).

## [17.0.1.0.291] — 2026-08-19 — `f071d7ed`

**Changed:**
- **View**: helpdesk_ticket_studio_ported.xml (+205/-294)

**Message:**
> wave 5A hardened re-emit: sanitized forms via port_studio_views.py
>
> Uses new scripts/dump_dev_fields.py output (data/dev_model_fields.json,
> 259 dev models) as pre-emit orphan-field filter in port_studio_views.py
> strip_orphan_fields(). Also skips 13 TransientModel wizard views and
> validates emitted arch via ET.fromstring before writing (rejects malformed).
> Regenerated all 179 form views. Baseline hand-crafted files (non-
> _studio_ported.xml suffix) still protected.

## [17.0.1.0.288] — 2026-08-18 — `e7e9ac8b`

**Changed:**

- **Model/field**: account_move.py (+8/-4)

**Message:**
> fix(v288): narrow v286 settle guard -- only fire on explicit rug_acc_updated=True
>
> v286 raised UserError('Cannot settle RUG invoice ... SO must be locked')
> on ANY write to a posted RUG invoice that touched any of:
>     {rug_confirmed, rug_acc_updated, state, payment_state}
> Fine on manual settle attempts. Broken once v287's SO -> invoice
> compute propagation ran: setting SO.rug_confirmed cascaded a stored-
> compute write on the invoice's rug_confirmed, which the v286 guard
> saw as "trigger touched" and raised.
> v288 tightens the guard to only fire on:
>     vals.get('x_studio_rug_acc_updated') is True
> That matches Studio's actual intent -- the automation was written
> to fire when the accounts team clicks 'Change to RUG Account' and
> sets rug_acc_updated to True on a posted RUG invoice. Background
> compute cascades that only touch rug_confirmed no longer trip it.
> Immediate symptom fixed: opening invoice FTI/2026/00001 (SO 81) no
> longer raises "SO must be locked" because SO 81 is intentionally
> unlocked in the test harness state.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 35 insertions(+), 5 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.287] — 2026-08-18 — `6c613a03`

**Changed:**

- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+19/-0)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.287 post-migration.py (+36/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v287): x_studio_rug_confirmed compute reactivity to SO-side flips
>
> The v281 stored compute on account.move only depends on invoice_origin
> + move_type. It walks invoice_origin (Char) -> sale.order by name
> match, but Odoo's ORM can't trace that string-based reverse lookup
> as a dependency. Flipping x_studio_rug_confirmed on the SO left the
> invoice's cached value stale.
> Symptom: SO 81 -> True; invoice FTI/2026/00001 stayed False; the
> automation 215 write-override guard skipped and no UserError fired.
> Fix: sale.order.write() override now calls
> _compute_x_studio_rug_confirmed on every invoice whose invoice_origin
> matches an SO whose flag just changed.
> Migration script back-fills existing invoices so cached values sync
> with current SO state on upgrade.

**Changed:**

- **Model/field**: account_move.py (+10/-7)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.286 post-migration.py (+18/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v286): Odoo-17 gap in RUG-invoice validation -- watch `locked` not `state=='done'`
>
> Same fix pattern as v285 for the Track Lock Status trio. My v281
> port of Studio automation 215 faithfully reproduced the guard
> `so.state != 'done'`, which never matches on Odoo 17 (states are
> draft/sent/sale/cancel). The native lock semantic moved to a Boolean
> field `locked`.
> Bug on Clear-DB: automation 215 has been unreachable since the Odoo
> 17 upgrade -- any attempt to set both rug_confirmed + rug_acc_updated
> on a posted invoice would either raise UserError('Incomplete sales
> order') or silently no-op depending on the SO state.
> v286 swaps `so.state != 'done'` for `not so.locked` in the write()
> override so the auto-settle actually fires when the SO is locked
> (Odoo 17 equivalent of the pre-v17 done state).
> Clear-DB reference left unchanged.

## [17.0.1.0.285] — 2026-08-17 — `152fa212`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+37/-22)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.285 post-migration.py (+43/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v285): Odoo-17 gap in Track Lock Status port -- watch `locked` not `state`
>
> Studio automations 202/203 gated on sale.order.state == 'done'. Odoo
> 17 removed the `done` state (states are now draft/sent/sale/cancel)
> and replaced it with a native `locked` Boolean. The Studio code has
> been dead code on Clear-DB since the v17 upgrade -- no SO ever
> satisfied the guard so x_studio_locked / -unlocked /
> -re_estimate_count have been stuck at their defaults.
> Dev-env confirmation: 0 Repair SOs have x_studio_locked=True or
> x_studio_re_estimate_count > 0, whereas 1 SO has native locked=True.
> v285 rewrites both branches to watch `locked` instead. Semantics
> preserved:
>   * locked -> True: mirror x_studio_locked, stamp re_estimate_count
>   * locked -> False (with Studio lock still True): flip x_studio_unlocked
> Post-migration back-fills any already-locked Repair SO so its Studio
> lock fields align with the native locked field.
> Clear-DB reference intentionally left unchanged (the Studio
> automation is broken on Clear-DB too, but we don't modify
> Clear-DB -- fix lives only in Fix-repair's port).

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 81 insertions(+), 23 deletions(-)

- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.284 post-migration.py (+19/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v284): port 'RR - Validate Payment %' automation (action 2427)
>
> Port Studio automation 241 as an @api.onchange handler on
> account.payment. Belt-and-braces validator that catches direct
> form-edit payments bypassing the account.payment.register wizard
> (which Fix-repair already gates via _validate_repair_advance_threshold).
> Same threshold math as the wizard path: sums every posted payment
> against the SO, adds this draft's amount, compares against
> (so.amount_total * company.x_studio_advance_payment_ / 100). Raises
> UserError if projected total falls below threshold.
> Guards on state=draft + quotation_type=Repair + SO set + configured
> company % > 0. No recursion guard needed (onchange scope, no writes).
> Requires BugFix-Sales v45 for x_studio_sales_order and
> x_studio_quotation_type on account.payment.

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 105 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.283] — 2026-08-17 — `5f709fd6`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+59/-0), sale_order_line.py (+32/-0)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.283 post-migration.py (+31/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v283): port 'RR - Track Lock Status' trio (automations 202/203/204)
>
> Ports the Studio lock/unlock cycle for repair SOs to Python:
>   * automation 202 (RR - Track Lock Status): when a Repair SO reaches
>     state='done', lock it and sync the header re_estimate_count from
>     the most recent re-estimated line's per-line counter.
>   * automation 203 (RR - Track Lock Status - 2): when a locked Repair
>     SO drops back to state='sale', unlock it.
>   * automation 204 (RR - Track Lock Status - 3): sale.order.line
>     onchange -- when parent Repair SO is currently unlocked, mark the
>     edited line as re-estimated and stamp per-line counter to
>     (header.re_estimate_count + 1).
> Both 202 and 203 are folded into a single helper method
> _fix_repair_apply_track_lock_status invoked from create() and write()
> with a triggers-based guard so unrelated writes don't re-run the
> check. Recursion via write() is guarded by the _fix_repair_track_lock
> context flag.
> The line-level onchange reads header state via record.order_id.x_studio_xxx
> instead of declaring three redundant related fields on sale.order.line.
> Requires BugFix-Sales v44 for the two new line-level marker fields.
> Post-migration back-fills existing done-state Repair SOs (locking
> them with correct re_estimate_count from their most recent
> re-estimated line).

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 123 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

> of Studio's cosmetic payment_state='in_payment' flip — creates a

> isn't in state='done'.

> x_studio_rug_confirmed / x_studio_rug_rejected on the Register Payment
> button. Those two Studio fields live on sale.order (declared by
> BugFix-Sales v40/v41), NOT on account.move. On Clear-DB the arch
> loaded fine because studio_customization has declared them cross-model
> via magic; on dev env's clean account.move, OWL's evaluator couldn't
> resolve them and blew up the entire invoice form render.
> Fix: swap the expression to is_rug_invoice — a Boolean compute we
> already declare on account.move (line 48). Its @api.depends walks
> invoice_origin → sale.order and returns True iff the SO is
> quotation_type='Repair', not customer-pays, and not rug-rejected —
> the same predicate the buggy expression tried to encode
> (warranty repair invoice that will auto-settle via _rug_auto_settle
> on post, so no manual Register Payment is appropriate).

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 13 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.273] — 2026-08-13 — `9400655e`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+27/-5)

**Message:**
> fix(v273): same-warehouse fallback for received-at-factory + send-to-sales-centre
>
> On dev / demo envs where the centre and factory resolve to the same
> warehouse (e.g. My Company SF has only YourCompany/WH), the intransit
> hops collapse — factory.Intransit == centre.Intransit == current source
> — and _create_repair_transfer silently returns False. Stage still
> advances (action_* methods ignore the return value) but no picking is
> created, so the Movements smart button skips the step.
> Fix in _create_received_at_factory_picking and
> _create_send_to_sales_centre_picking: when the computed Intransit dest
> equals src, fall back to warehouse.lot_stock_id. The picking still
> gets recorded and the chain remains navigable.
> Only fires when src == dest_intransit. Clear-DB (centre BR-* !=
> factory PW-JM/RP-* always) never hits the fallback — behaviour there
> is unchanged.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 28 insertions(+), 6 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.272] — 2026-08-13 — `c5e40385`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+11/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.272 post-migration.py (+27/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+38/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v272): activate dormant internal picking types + tolerant search
>
> The Send to Factory / Received at Factory / Send to Sales Centre /
> Received at Sales Centre buttons silently no-op'd on the dev env
> after the initial Return picking. Stage advanced but no stock.picking
> was created — user reported "only 1st return movement works, rest isnt".
> Root cause: warehouse 1 (YourCompany)'s int_type_id (id 5, "Internal
> Transfers") shipped with active=False on the standalone Odoo Enterprise
> env. Odoo's ORM auto-filters active=True on searches, so
> _create_repair_transfer's PickType.search([('code','=','internal'), ...])
> returned empty and the function's `if not pick_type: return False`
> guard silently killed every subsequent picking. action_send_to_factory
> et al. ignore the return value, so the stage still advanced.
> Two-layer fix:
> 1. hooks.py: activate_internal_picking_types(env) flips active=True on
>    every stock.picking.type with code='internal' and warehouse_id set,
>    idempotently. Registered in post_init_hook and
>    migrations/17.0.1.0.272/post-migration.py.
> 2. helpdesk_ticket.py: _create_repair_transfer's PickType search now
>    uses .with_context(active_test=False) so it succeeds even if a
>    future warehouse ships with the type inactive.
> Both layers together — the seed handles the immediate case and the
> tolerant search prevents recurrence if a customer disables an internal
> type manually.

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 77 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

---

> _repair_studio_auto_create_repair_serial_nos (line 4042).

**Message:**
> fix(v255): stamp partner_id on auto-created repair-route picking
>
> _repair_studio_auto_create_repair_route was creating the synthetic
> outgoing picking without a partner_id, so helpdesk_stock's
> has_partner_picking compute never treated it as a real prior
> delivery to the ticket's partner. Return button stayed hidden on
> standalone even after the delegate ran successfully.
> Clear-DB masked this because their cash-customer aggregator has
> 1,200+ genuine done deliveries — has_partner_picking is always true
> there regardless of what the delegate creates. Standalone can't
> lean on that.
> Stamping partner_id = record.partner_id.id closes the gap: the
> compute finds a matching delivery for the ticket's partner, the
> Return button surfaces, and the Return → Send to Factory chain
> becomes reachable.

## [17.0.1.0.250] — 2026-08-12 — `dea7c429`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.250 pre-migration.py (+68/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v250): pre-migration script force-creates ir.model.data pins
>
> v249's data/ir_model_pins.xml did not resolve the Clear-DB upgrade
> failure. When Odoo's data-loader processes <record model="ir.model">
> for a row whose `model` field value already matches an existing
> ir.model row (as is the case for x_task_diagnosis + 10 other
> Studio-created models on Clear-DB), the loader silently reuses the
> existing row but does NOT create a companion ir.model.data pin.
> Result: pin still missing, security/ir.model.access.csv still fails
> with "No matching record found for external id 'model_x_task_diagnosis'".
> New approach: migrations/17.0.1.0.250/pre-migration.py runs BEFORE
> load_data — the only phase where the pin can be created ahead of
> the CSV loader looking for it. Uses raw SQL INSERT ... ON CONFLICT
> DO NOTHING on ir_model_data so already-pinned rows are left alone
> (idempotent on stand-alone; effective on Clear-DB).
> For each of the 11 target models (x_conditions, x_symptom_areas,
> x_symptom_codes, x_diagnosis_areas, x_diagnosis_codes, x_resolutions,
> x_repair_accounts, x_repair_reason, x_repair_reason_custom,
> x_repair_stages, x_repair_sub_reason, x_task_diagnosis):
>   1. Look up the ir_model.id via `SELECT id FROM ir_model WHERE model = %s`
>   2. If not found (fresh install path — model will be created next
>      phase): skip; the ORM's model-registration will create the pin
>   3. If found: INSERT into ir_model_data with module='Fix-repair',
>      name='model_<model>', model='ir.model', res_id=<found id>,
>      noupdate=TRUE. ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING protects against re-runs.
> Rationale for raw SQL over ORM:
>   - Pre-migration doesn't have a fully-usable env yet (env.registry
>     is being rebuilt)
>   - Raw SQL sidesteps the special ir.model.data _update semantics
>     that were the root cause of v249's silent skip
> data/ir_model_pins.xml (v249) is retained but now redundant —
> harmless because our records match existing rows and update
> in-place. Left in place for the fresh-install path where it may
> provide a fallback if pre-migration doesn't fire.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 69 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.249] — 2026-08-12 — `b5390ef5`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+8/-1)
- **ir.model pins**: +ir_model_pins.xml (+93/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v249): force ir.model xmlid pins for 11 Studio-created models
>
> Fix upgrade error on Clear-DB:
>   Module loading Fix-repair failed: file security/ir.model.access.csv
>   could not be processed:
>    No matching record found for external id 'model_x_task_diagnosis'
>    in field 'Model'
>    Missing required value for the field 'Model' (model_id)
> Root cause: on stand-alone, Odoo's pre-init phase auto-creates the
> `model_<model>` ir.model.data pin when a module declares a new
> models.Model class in Python (fresh install path). On Clear-DB the
> same models pre-existed as state='manual' Studio rows created by
> studio_customization; when Fix-repair v227/v241 later declared the
> Python classes, the state flipped 'manual' -> 'base' but the
> ir.model.data pin was NEVER auto-created (that only fires for
> freshly-registered ir.model rows).
> Result: the ACL CSV loader tries env.ref('model_x_task_diagnosis'),
> finds no ir.model.data with that name in any module, and fails.
> Fix: new data/ir_model_pins.xml with 11 explicit `<record model="ir.model">`
> entries — one per Studio-created model Fix-repair now owns:
>   - Chunk 1a (v241): 6 diagnosis catalogues (x_conditions,
>     x_symptom_areas, x_symptom_codes, x_diagnosis_areas,
>     x_diagnosis_codes, x_resolutions)
>   - Pre-Chunk-1a (v227): 5 repair-master models (x_repair_accounts,
>     x_repair_reason, x_repair_reason_custom, x_repair_stages,
>     x_repair_sub_reason)
>   - v227: x_task_diagnosis (the one that triggered this failure)
> Loaded BEFORE security/ir.model.access.csv in the manifest 'data'
> list. Using bare `<record>` with just name+model fields — Odoo
> find-or-creates the ir.model row by name, updates in place if
> pre-existing (state stays 'base'), creates cleanly if absent.
> Idempotent.
> On stand-alone this file is redundant (the pins auto-existed) but
> harmless — data-XML load semantics update-in-place matches what
> was already there. On Clear-DB it creates the missing pins so the
> ACL CSV loads successfully.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 101 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

>    fix_repair.x_diagnosis_codes_58 for BRNT-COIL).
>    noupdate="1" — subsequent Fix-repair upgrades will NOT overwrite
>    user edits. Users can freely rename/delete via UI without

>   2. Open Helpdesk app -> top-nav shows "Repair Diagnosis" dropdown

## [17.0.1.0.245] — 2026-08-12 — `19363d22`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+2/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+12/-0)
- **Data records (XML)**: +repair_sequences.xml (+42/-0)

**Message:**
> feat(v245): Chunk 1f — port repair.seq + default name='New' for auto ticket ID
>
> User feedback: "in repair test there is no field for helpdesk.ticket.name
> and maybe doesn't have the automation to automatically create the ID".
> Verified against Clear-DB:
>   - Recent tickets: "REPAIR/2026/01228", ..., "REPAIR/2026/01209"
>   - ir.default id=301 sets helpdesk.ticket.name = '"New"' by default
>   - ir.sequence id=811: code='repair.seq', prefix='REPAIR/%(year)s/',
>     padding=5
>   - ir.actions.server id=1976 "RR - Repair Seq.No" runs
>     `record._repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write()`
>   - That method already lives in Fix-repair helpdesk_ticket.py
>     (line 2695) and reads env['ir.sequence'].next_by_code('repair.seq').
> Two missing pieces on stand-alone:
> 1. **ir.sequence records absent.** next_by_code returns False, so
>    the name stays 'New' after create and the ticket is unidentifiable.
>    New file data/repair_sequences.xml seeds:
>      - repair.seq        (Repair Sequence No, prefix REPAIR/%(year)s/,
>                           padding 5, id fix_repair.seq_repair_ticket)
>      - repair.serial.seq (Repair Serial Sequence No, prefix
>                           REP-SERIAL/%(year)s/, padding 5)
> 2. **No default on required 'name' field.** Odoo raises "Invalid
>    fields: Subject" toast on save when name is empty. Added
>    `name = fields.Char(default='New')` to the HelpdeskTicket class
>    in models/helpdesk_ticket.py. Doubles as the sentinel value
>    _repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write() looks for.
> Together these restore Clear-DB's auto-name behaviour:
>   1. User creates ticket → name defaults to 'New' → save succeeds
>   2. create() override calls _repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write()
>   3. That method sees name=='New', pulls next_by_code('repair.seq')
>      → assigns 'REPAIR/2026/00001'
>   4. Second ticket gets 'REPAIR/2026/00002', etc.
> noupdate="1" on repair_sequences.xml so subsequent upgrades don't
> reset number_next back to 1.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 56 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.244] — 2026-08-12 — `e6772ad0`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+11/-6)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.244 post-migration.py (+16/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+10/-20)

**Message:**
> fix(v244): move ported view files back to manifest 'data' so they survive upgrade cleanup
>
> v243 upgrade succeeded and post_init_hook re-created the 3 ported
> helpdesk.ticket view records — but the very next upgrade cycle
> (the v243 one itself, at its end) purged them again. Odoo's
> ir.model.data._process_end() runs at the tail of each module
> upgrade and deletes any ir.model.data row where module=<current
> module> and the xmlid wasn't touched during THIS cycle. Records
> created via convert_file inside a post-migration script are NOT
> registered in that cycle's "touched" set, so they get purged as
> orphans.
> Symptom (verified 2026-08-12 09:07): after v243 upgrade,
>   ir.model.data.search([
>       ('module','=','Fix-repair'),
>       ('model','=','ir.ui.view'),
>       ('name','like','view_helpdesk_ticket')
>   ])
> returns 0 rows. Ticket form on stand-alone reverted to plain layout
> (no repair fields, no Warranty Details / Cancel-Reopen Log tabs).
> Fix: move the two view files out of hooks._STUDIO_DEPENDENT_VIEW_FILES
> and into __manifest__.py 'data' list. The button-195 xpath that
> originally forced the post-init route (v234) was stripped in v242,
> so manifest-data loading works cleanly now — Odoo composes the
> parent view with every module's inherit applied by the time
> Fix-repair's data files load, so //button[@name='195'] is no longer
> referenced anywhere and no init-time validation errors.
> Load order in manifest 'data':
>   1. helpdesk_ticket_views.xml    (existing Fix-repair inherits)
>   2. helpdesk_ticket_studio_ported.xml  (adds ~90 Studio field
>                                          placements + tabs)
>   3. helpdesk_ticket_studio_field_hides.xml  (hides 9 of those
>                                               fields — targets
>                                               elements ported.xml
>                                               added, so must load
>                                               after)
> hooks._STUDIO_DEPENDENT_VIEW_FILES is emptied but the machinery
> (load_post_init_view_files) kept as a hook for any future file that
> truly must load post-init.
> Also adds a no-op migrations/17.0.1.0.244/post-migration.py so the
> version bump processes cleanly.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 37 insertions(+), 26 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.243] — 2026-08-12 — `7744051e`

**Message:**
> feat(v243): Chunk 1d — seed 12 repair-pipeline helpdesk.stage records
>
> After v242 gave stand-alone the ticket form arch, the statusbar
> still showed the default Odoo stages (New, In Progress, On Hold,
> Solved, Canceled). Fix-repair's Python code (state transitions,
> _get_view invisible expressions, button-visibility logic) all
> depends on tickets moving through the repair pipeline:
>   New → Sent to Factory → Received at Factory → Diagnosis →
>   Estimation Sent to Customer → Estimation Approval Received →
>   Advance Received (fold) → Repair Started → Repair Completed →
>   Sent to Sales Centre → Received at Sales Centre (fold) →
>   Handed Over to Customer (fold) → Cancelled (fold)
> This chunk seeds the 12 new stages (reusing the default "New") and
> attaches them to every existing helpdesk.team so they show up on
> statusbars immediately.
> Files:
>   data/repair_stages.xml — 12 <record> for helpdesk.stage. xmlids
>     fix_repair.stage_<snake_case_name>. Sequences 10-120 in steps
>     of 10 (leaves default stages at 0-4). fold=True on Advance
>     Received, Received at Sales Centre, Handed Over, Cancelled.
>     No team_ids in XML (attached by hook so the seed doesn't fail
>     on DBs without teams yet).
>   hooks.py — new function attach_repair_stages_to_all_teams(env)
>     that reads the 12 seeded stages via env.ref() and appends each
>     helpdesk.team.id to their team_ids m2m. Idempotent via set()
>     diff. Called from post_init_hook (install) and from the v243
>     migration (upgrade).
>   __manifest__.py — bump 242 → 243, register data/repair_stages.xml
>   migrations/17.0.1.0.243/post-migration.py — invoke the team hook
>     on the upgrade path
> Effect on stand-alone: 12 new stages appear in Configuration → Stages,
> each attached to both existing teams (Customer Care, VIP Support).
> Ticket statusbar shows the full repair pipeline after refresh.
> Non-goals (deferred):
>   - Archiving default helpdesk stages (In Progress etc.) — user can
>     do this from UI if desired
>   - Company scoping (x_studio_company_id) — a later chunk when
>     multi-company config is needed

## [17.0.1.0.240] — 2026-08-12 — `119c93e5`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.240 post-migration.py (+21/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+4/-1)

**Message:**
> fix(v240): cast arch_db::text before LIKE — jsonb operator error
>
> v239 sentinel SQL:
>   SELECT COUNT(1) FROM ir_ui_view
>   WHERE model='helpdesk.ticket' AND active IS TRUE
>   AND arch_db LIKE '%name="195"%'
> blew up with:
>   psycopg2.errors.UndefinedFunction: operator does not exist:
>   jsonb ~~ unknown
> because in Odoo 17 ir_ui_view.arch_db is stored as jsonb (Odoo's
> translated-field mechanism). Postgres has no LIKE operator on jsonb.
> Fix: arch_db::text LIKE '%name="195"%'. Casting to text serialises
> the jsonb which produces something like {"en_US":"<...>"} — the
> literal name="195" still appears inside so the LIKE matches on
> Clear-DB. On stand-alone the same query returns 0 (button is
> name="853"), sentinel returns False, view file skipped.
> Sanity: python -m ast parses hooks.py cleanly.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 26 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.238] — 2026-08-12 — `905971b1`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **DB migration**: +17.0.1.0.238 post-migration.py (+22/-0)
- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+41/-24)

**Message:**
> fix(v238): sentinel-gate ported view files on Studio-action-ID presence
>
> v237 upgrade migration failed with:
>   ParseError: Element '<xpath expr="//button[@name='195']">'
>   cannot be located in parent view
>   file: helpdesk_ticket_studio_ported.xml
> Root cause: Studio hardcoded database-specific numeric action IDs into
> view arches. View #4012 references `//button[@name='195']` because on
> Clear-DB (where Studio was set up), the helpdesk_stock Return button
> had `name="195"`. On stand-alone Odoo installs the same button was
> allocated action ID 853 by Odoo when helpdesk_stock installed on the
> fresh DB. Xpath can't find button 195 on stand-alone → validation
> aborts.
> Interim fix: convert _STUDIO_DEPENDENT_VIEW_FILES from list-of-paths
> to list-of-(path, sentinel_callable) tuples. Each entry gets a
> callable that returns True when the file is safe to load on this DB.
> Both currently-listed files get a sentinel that checks
> `ir.actions.actions.browse(195).exists()` — True on Clear-DB,
> False on stand-alone.
> Effect:
>   - Clear-DB: sentinel True → both files load, form matches
>     production as before
>   - Stand-alone: sentinel False → both files skip, form stays at
>     base + core Python inherits, upgrade completes cleanly
> Real fix (deferred): port the Studio Return action as a proper
> Fix-repair Python ir.actions.server with a stable xmlid, then
> rewrite the view arch to reference it via
> `%(fix_repair.action_studio_return)d`. That makes both envs use
> the same-named action even though the numeric IDs differ.
> Tracked as follow-up.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 64 insertions(+), 25 deletions(-)

> stayed in the Clear-DB records only — never landing in any on-disk XML
> file. Result: fresh Fix-repair installs never re-create these views,
> so the ticket form loses all Studio-added field placements, tabs and
> groupings, showing only base helpdesk arch + Fix-repair's Python
> inherits.

>         (Odoo Studio: helpdesk.ticket.form customization)

>         (Odoo Studio: helpdesk.ticket.form-button)

>   #5027 view_helpdesk_ticket_tree_5027     tree   ext   1,018 bytes
> Each <record> reuses the exact xmlid Fix-repair.ir.model.data already
> has for the DB view, so on Clear-DB upgrade the existing row is
> updated in-place (same arch → no functional change); on stand-alone
> install the row is created fresh with the correct arch.

> Python-declared in Fix-repair (audited 2026-08-12 against
> ir.model.fields on Clear-DB — every one has state='base' and
> modules='Fix-repair'). No extra field declarations needed.
> XML validates via python -m xml.etree.ElementTree parse.

> res.users (2), helpdesk.stage (2), helpdesk.ticket.type (1),
> plus the 4-5 x_repair_* extension views. Land as separate commits
> once helpdesk.ticket render is verified on both envs.

## [17.0.1.0.232] — 2026-08-12 — `b0fcb430`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+23/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v232): inject sentinel fields for header-button invisible expressions
>
> Runtime OwlError at form render on standalone installs:
>   EvalError: Can not evaluate python expression:
>     (bool(not use_fsm or fsm_task_count > 0 or
>     (x_studio_rug_repair and not x_studio_valid_return) or
>     (x_studio_job_location == 'Factory Repair' and repair_stage_state != 'received_at_factory') or
>     (x_studio_job_location == 'Centre Repair' and (repair_stage_state != 'new' or not has_return_picking)) or
>     not x_studio_job_location))
>   Error: Name 'x_studio_rug_repair' is not defined
> Odoo's client-side modifier evaluator only sees fields that appear
> somewhere in the view arch (visible or invisible). On the Jinasena
> production DB Studio's own view inherits include these fields visibly;
> on stand-alone installs the base helpdesk.ticket arch has none of them,
> so any button whose `invisible=` expression references them blows up
> at render.
> Fix: extend the existing sentinel-field injection loop in _get_view
> to add all fields named in the header-button modifiers. Sentinels
> are inserted as `<field invisible="1"/>` — they carry no visual
> weight but ensure the values reach the client.
> New sentinels:
>   x_studio_rug_repair, x_studio_rug_confirmed, x_studio_valid_return,
>   x_studio_serial_no, x_studio_repair_reason, x_studio_warranty_card,
>   x_studio_pick_id, repair_stage_state, use_fsm, fsm_task_count.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 24 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.230] — 2026-08-12 — `fd8b498c`

**Changed:**

- **Install hooks**: hooks.py (+12/-4)

**Message:**
> fix(v230): switch conditional-view sentinel to studio_customization presence
>
> The v228 sentinel `helpdesk.ticket.x_studio_rug_repair in
> ir.model.fields` was wrong. Fix-repair itself declares
> x_studio_rug_repair as a Python field (state='base'), so the check
> returned True even on stand-alone DBs where studio_customization
> is absent. That defeated the whole guard — the view file loaded
> and xpath still failed to locate x_studio_rug_repair in the base
> view's arch, because Studio wasn't there to inject it.
> Fix: check the studio_customization module itself
> (ir.module.module state == 'installed'). That's the only reliable
> signal that the parent view arch contains Studio-added fields.
> Renamed the constant _CONDITIONAL_DATA -> _STUDIO_DEPENDENT_VIEW_FILES
> since it's now just a list of file paths — the sentinel is uniform.
>   - Jinasena production: studio_customization is installed → all
>     Studio-dependent view hides load as before.
>   - Stand-alone Odoo: studio_customization isn't installed → hides
>     are skipped, install completes.

>
> The ticket `name` field displays as REPAIR/YYYY/NNNNN, assigned at
> create() by _repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write via the repair.seq
> per-company sequence. But the form arch left the field editable, so
> typing anything into the title on the New ticket page silently beat
> the sequence hook (which only fires when the value is 'New' or empty)
> and left the ticket with an arbitrary human-typed name.
> Two-part lock:
>   1. _get_view: set readonly=1 on //field[@name='name']. The New
>      ticket form now shows the field but disables the input — nothing
>      to type, nothing to override.
>   2. _repair_seq_no_on_create_or_write: broaden the trigger from
>      `== 'New'` alone to `not record.name or == 'New'`. With the
>      field readonly, users can no longer force the 'New' sentinel by
>      typing over the initial default; catching the empty case too
>      ensures the sequence still fires whatever the initial state is.
> Version 17.0.1.0.219 -> 17.0.1.0.220.

## [17.0.1.0.215] — 2026-07-28 — `84a84899`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+57/-3), stock_return_picking.py (+27/-16)

**Message:**
> feat(v215): one-click Dispatch — skip the wizard for the send-back step too
>
> Symmetric to v209's Return retargeting. The Dispatch header button
> now calls a new fix_repair_action_direct_dispatch method on
> helpdesk.ticket instead of opening action 195. Behaviour mirrors the
> interactive Dispatch flow:
>   - ticket_id is deliberately left unset on the wizard, so Studio
>     automation 174's `if record.ticket_id:` guard skips its
>     "Return Location should be equal to Suggested Return Location"
>     check — same trick the interactive button relied on
>   - default_picking_id is the ticket's stored x_studio_pick_id (the
>     RET picking from the earlier collection), so the wizard reverses
>     that instead of building a phantom
>   - default_location_id is deliberately NOT set — we don't want to
>     trigger the "provided-value skips the compute" trap here either;
>     Odoo's standard _compute_moves_locations correctly sets
>     location_id to picking_id.location_id = Customers for a reversal
>     of the incoming RET picking (the item goes back to the customer)
>   - Precondition raise: if x_studio_pick_id isn't set (no earlier
>     collection recorded), we raise with a clear message instead of
>     tripping downstream
> To make Odoo's standard location compute survive intact for Dispatch,
> _compute_moves_locations is now gated on wizard.ticket_id. Without
> that gate, our override would keep clobbering location_id with the
> ticket's suggested repair location (via original_location_id
> fallback), pointing the "back-to-customer" reversal at the repair
> site instead — subtly wrong even if the automation didn't catch it.
> Since automation 174 also gates on ticket_id, the two now line up:
> we only massage location_id where the automation would also validate.
> Version 17.0.1.0.214 -> 17.0.1.0.215.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 85 insertions(+), 20 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.214] — 2026-07-28 — `cbf638e0`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+10/-5)

**Message:**
> fix(v214): with_context(ctx) positionally — the pop needs a replace, not merge
>
> v213's ctx.pop('default_location_id', None) was a no-op through the
> subsequent .with_context(**ctx) call. **kwargs to with_context MERGES
> into env.context — so the default_location_id: False that came in via
> the button's context attribute stayed in env.context even after I
> popped it from the local ctx dict, and Odoo's default_get still saw
> False and skipped _compute_moves_locations.
> Verified via RPC: reproducing the button click on v213 by passing
> {default_location_id: False, ...} in the ctx returned exactly the
> user's error string. The one-line fix — using with_context(ctx)
> POSITIONALLY, which REPLACES env.context — makes the pop actually
> take effect. The compute now runs, populates product_return_moves,
> sets location_id to the company-appropriate suggested location, and
> Studio automation 174 validates against a matching pair.
> Version 17.0.1.0.213 -> 17.0.1.0.214.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 11 insertions(+), 6 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.213] — 2026-07-28 — `a85a3058`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+21/-0)

**Message:**
> fix(v213): strip default_location_id from button-inherited context
>
> The header button's context attribute — retained from the pre-v209
> action-195 setup for backward-compat with the Dispatch sibling that
> still opens the standard wizard — passes
> default_location_id: (ship_back_cond and cust_loc_id) or False.
> In the New stage (the case that triggers Return, not Dispatch),
> ship_back_cond evaluates to False, so the button sends
> default_location_id: False.
> My method inherited env.context wholesale, so
> stock.return.picking.create({}) received default_location_id=False.
> Odoo's ORM interprets a value in vals for a stored @api.depends
> compute field as "user-provided" and SKIPS the compute — same trap
> that broke v210. Location_id stayed at False, _compute_moves_locations
> never ran, product_return_moves stayed empty, and automation 174
> raised "Return Location should be equal to Suggested Return Location."
> The bug only showed up through the UI because RPC callers (like my
> verification probes) never carried the button context — env.context
> was minimal and the leak didn't happen. Classic UI-only regression.
> Fix: ctx.pop('default_location_id', None) right after copying
> env.context. Compute now runs in full, sets location_id to the
> company-appropriate suggested location, and the automation passes.
> Verified via RPC by injecting the exact button context
> (default_location_id: False) and confirming the pre-fix path
> reproduced the user's error string verbatim.
> Version 17.0.1.0.212 -> 17.0.1.0.213.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 22 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

## [17.0.1.0.212] — 2026-07-28 — `5179431b`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+5/-1)

**Message:**
> fix(v212): x_studio_pick_id is an integer field, not a many2one
>
> helpdesk.ticket.x_studio_pick_id is a Studio integer column that
> stores the raw picking id (0 when unset). My v209 direct-return
> method called .id on it — which works for m2o records but raises
> AttributeError: 'int' object has no attribute 'id' when the ticket
> had a picking recorded from a previous return round.
> The bug lay dormant on fresh tickets (where the value is 0 and the
> truthiness check short-circuits), but any second Return click after
> v209 wrote a picking id back via _create_returns tripped it — which
> also explains why the same ticket showed "still has the issue" after
> each successive push: the ticket had accumulated an x_studio_pick_id
> from the previously-succeeded create_returns.
> Fix: pass the raw integer via `self.x_studio_pick_id or False`,
> mirroring exactly what the v199 header button context expression did.

## [17.0.1.0.211] — 2026-07-28 — `97ab127d`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+12/-29), stock_return_picking.py (+27/-6)

**Message:**
> fix(v211): let the compute run, and mirror the automation's company branch
>
> v210 was worse than v209. Passing default_location_id in ctx made Odoo
> skip the entire _compute_moves_locations run — location_id was set fine
> from the provided value, but the sibling fields the same compute
> populates (notably product_return_moves) stayed empty, and then
> create_returns tripped stock's "Please specify at least one non-zero
> quantity" check.
> Two changes:
> 1. helpdesk_ticket.fix_repair_action_direct_return no longer sets
>    default_location_id. The compute runs in full, populates
>    product_return_moves off the phantom picking, and the same compute
>    override below sets location_id to the correct suggested location.
>    Verified end-to-end via RPC on ticket 1891: wizard.location_id
>    lands at ticket.x_studio_virtual_location_1 (matches the
>    automation), product_return_moves populated with quantity=1,
>    create_returns returns a valid act_window on the new RET picking.
> 2. stock_return_picking._compute_moves_locations is now strictly
>    company-aware, matching the automation branch:
>      Company 1 → x_studio_suggested_location_id (or original)
>      Other      → x_studio_suggested_location_id_1 (or original)
>    Previous "_1 or _id" fallback silently picked the wrong sister-field
>    when a user profile carried both (per-company variants of the same
>    virtual location tree), leaving the automation to raise "Return
>    Location should be equal to Suggested Return Location". The
>    automation now sees location_id set to exactly the field it checks
>    against.
> Also removed the fix_repair_direct_return context bypass added in an
> intermediate step — it's no longer needed since default_location_id is
> gone and the compute runs unconditionally.
> Version 17.0.1.0.210 -> 17.0.1.0.211.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 40 insertions(+), 36 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.210] — 2026-07-27 — `70c723e4`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+30/-19)

**Message:**
> fix(v210): align Return Location with Suggested — the automation runs alongside create
>
> Studio automation 174 (on_create_or_write of stock.return.picking, fires
> server action 1991) raises "Return Location should be equal to Suggested
> Return Location" when they differ. Its branches:
>   Company 1 → location_id must equal x_studio_suggested_location_id
>               (related: ticket_id.x_studio_virtual_location)
>   Other      → location_id must equal x_studio_suggested_location_id_1
>               (related: ticket_id.x_studio_virtual_location_1)
> The interactive wizard survived this because _compute_moves_locations
> aligned location_id to suggested before the user clicked Return. Our
> direct create() invokes the automation in the same transaction as the
> compute — Odoo's ordering isn't guaranteed to run the compute first,
> so the automation validated a stale location_id and raised.
> Fix: precompute the suggested location on the ticket side using the
> automation's own company branch, and pass it as default_location_id in
> context. default_get returns it, create writes it, automation
> validates against it → passes. No reliance on compute ordering.
> Removed the previous ship_back → Customers default_location_id branch:
> the compute always overwrote it anyway (dead code carried over from
> mirroring the header button context).
> Version 17.0.1.0.209 -> 17.0.1.0.210.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 31 insertions(+), 20 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.209] — 2026-07-27 — `ddd39449`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+84/-0)

**Message:**
> feat(v209): one-click Return — skip the stock.return.picking wizard popup
>
> Repair tickets always have deterministic wizard defaults — default_get
> on stock.return.picking already synthesises the phantom source picking,
> product_return_moves is auto-computed off it, and quantity is capped to
> 1 by _compute_moves_locations. Nothing on the wizard needs a user
> decision, so opening the modal and asking for a second Return click is
> pure friction.
> The Return button on the helpdesk ticket now calls a new
> fix_repair_action_direct_return() method on helpdesk.ticket instead of
> opening action 195. The method:
>   - Rebuilds the same context the button used to pass
>     (default_ticket_id, default_picking_id, default_partner_id,
>     default_location_id when hand-back-to-customer, default_company_id)
>   - Instantiates a stock.return.picking record in memory (default_get
>     fires and builds the phantom, sets picking_id, fills product_return_moves)
>   - Calls the wizard's public create_returns() which invokes our
>     _create_returns() override (renames to <WH>/RET/xxxxx, stamps the
>     ticket, forces to_refund=False, pre-populates the serial) and
>     returns the ir.actions.act_window opening the new RET picking
> Net effect: click Return on the ticket → land on the freshly-created
> RET picking. One click, no wizard.
> Scope: only the Return button is retargeted. The Dispatch sibling
> (added by _get_view later in the same block) still points at action 195
> — its flow starts from a real source picking already selected, so the
> wizard preview has slightly different UX shape. Can be flipped to the
> same method in a follow-up if the user wants a matching Dispatch UX.

## [17.0.1.0.208] — 2026-07-27 — `6ed2edb1`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+7/-1)
- **Model/field**: project_task.py (+26/-15)
- **Static assets**: +static/src/js/task_missing_data_notifier.js (+99/-0), +static/src/scss/task_toast_triggers.scss (+12/-0)

**Message:**
> feat(v208): toasts driven by arch reactivity — best of both
>
> User wants toasts back but with the reliable dismiss behaviour
> v207's inline banners had. Combine both approaches:
>   1. Keep the arch <div> injections in project_task._get_view,
>      preserving the invisible="..." expressions that mirror the
>      original Studio-button visibility. Odoo's built-in arch
>      reactivity governs when these divs are present in the DOM.
>   2. Style the divs display:none via a small SCSS asset — they
>      never render as visible banners. Users never see them.
>   3. New service task_missing_data_notifier.js registers a
>      MutationObserver on document.body. When a trigger div
>      appears in the DOM (invisible expression flipped False —
>      data missing) it dispatches a sticky warning notification.
>      When the div disappears (expression True — data present)
>      it programmatically dismisses the notification.
> Why this succeeds where v205/v206's useEffect failed: arch
> invisible re-evaluates on EVERY field write, including the
> binary image widget's silent updates. We're not observing the
> record's data directly; we're observing the DOM presence of the
> arch's own reactive output. Odoo owns that pipeline.
> Class markers on the trigger divs:
>   * fix_repair_task_toast_trigger--diagnosis
>   * fix_repair_task_toast_trigger--image
> Sharing the parent class fix_repair_task_toast_trigger so the
> CSS hide-rule applies to both.
> Assets manifest expanded again — the SCSS + JS asset bundle
> was dropped in v207 (banners needed no JS). Restored here with
> the two new files.
> Version 17.0.1.0.207 → 17.0.1.0.208.

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 144 insertions(+), 16 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.207] — 2026-07-27 — `7eb5208d`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-6)
- **Model/field**: project_task.py (+57/-0)
- **Static assets**: -static/src/js/task_missing_data_notifier.js (+0/-128)

**Message:**
> feat(v207): inline banners replace flaky JS toast — arch reactivity
>
> v190→v206 built up a JS notification chain to auto-dismiss the
> Add Data toasts on field change. Every attempt (onRecordSaved →
> onPatched → useEffect on reactive proxy deps) missed the image
> upload path because Odoo's binary field widget mutates the
> record silently — not through the render loop, not through
> the standard reactive-proxy Set trap the effect could track.
> User's suggestion — mirror the ORIGINAL button visibility logic
> — is the right shape. Odoo's arch-level invisible expressions
> re-evaluate on every field change through the framework's own
> built-in reactivity, no custom JS required. The two Studio
> buttons (removed in v190) worked exactly the same way; the
> notification path we tried was just a worse re-implementation
> of that pattern.
> Rewrite as inline alert-warning divs injected into the task
> form's sheet by project_task._get_view. Visibility mirrors the
> original buttons' invisible expressions:
>     not helpdesk_ticket_id           — non-repair task
>     or x_studio_end_quick_repair     — quick repair path
>     or x_studio_cancelled            — cancelled task
>     or <the specific field>          — data already present
> Banners text:
>   * "Add Data: Repair Diagnosis Validation is not set for this task."
>   * "Add Data: Repair Image is not set for this task."
> Idempotent: skips injection if an alert with the same name is
> already present in the arch (subsequent _get_view calls on the
> same arch, e.g. Studio's own re-computes).
> Also:
>   * Delete static/src/js/task_missing_data_notifier.js — v190
>     through v206's failed JS path.
>   * Drop the assets bundle from __manifest__ — no JS assets left
>     to bundle.
> Version 17.0.1.0.206 → 17.0.1.0.207.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 58 insertions(+), 134 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.206] — 2026-07-27 — `9c1d4726`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Static assets**: static/src/js/task_missing_data_notifier.js (+29/-13)

**Message:**
> fix(v206): tie Add Data auto-dismiss to record.data changes directly
>
> v205's onPatched hook fires only when OWL re-renders the form.
> Some field widgets (binary image, especially) update the record's
> data silently — no render triggered — so the toast dismisser
> never fired until save.
> Switch to Odoo's useEffect (from @web/core/utils/hooks) with a
> deps callback that reads the three watched values through OWL's
> reactive proxy:
>     () => [
>         record.data.helpdesk_ticket_id,
>         record.data.x_studio_valid_diagnosis,
>         record.data.x_studio_repair_image_01,
>     ]
> Because the deps callback reads through the reactive proxy,
> useEffect tracks those specific record slots and re-runs the
> effect the moment ANY of them change — regardless of whether a
> render happens. helpdesk_ticket_id is watched too so scope
> changes (e.g., record swap) re-evaluate correctly.
> Existing hooks preserved:
>   * onMounted        — first check when the form appears.
>   * onRecordSaved    — safety re-check after remote data
>                        mutations that Odoo re-fetches.
>   * onWillUnmount    — clear all dismissers on unmount.
> Removed:
>   * onPatched — replaced by useEffect, which is more precise.
> Version 17.0.1.0.205 → 17.0.1.0.206.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 30 insertions(+), 14 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.205] — 2026-07-27 — `7f5417eb`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Static assets**: static/src/js/task_missing_data_notifier.js (+13/-1)

**Message:**
> feat(v205): auto-dismiss Add Data toasts on field change (no save needed)
>
> v190 dispatched the two Add Data toasts (missing diagnosis,
> missing repair image) on task-form mount, then re-checked via
> onRecordSaved — meaning the toasts only disappeared once the
> salesperson explicitly saved. User wants them to close as soon as
> the corresponding field is populated, without needing a save.
> Add an onPatched hook on the FormController patch for
> project.task forms. onPatched fires after every render, and
> OWL patches the DOM whenever record.data changes — so the
> moment the salesperson types the diagnosis or uploads the
> repair image, this hook runs and calls
> _fixRepairCheckMissingData again. The existing sync-notification
> logic then dismisses whichever toast just became irrelevant.
> Idempotent via the per-controller dismisser map: subsequent
> renders where nothing changed reach _fixRepairSyncNotification
> with shouldShow === (dismisser is set) and no-op — no duplicate
> toasts, no dismisser churn.
> Existing hooks preserved:
>   * onMounted — first check when the form appears.
>   * onRecordSaved — extra safety re-check after save (covers
>     remote data mutations Odoo re-fetches).
>   * onWillUnmount — clear all dismissers when leaving the form.
> Version 17.0.1.0.204 → 17.0.1.0.205.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 14 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.196] — 2026-07-23 — `5138d71f`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: account_move.py (+28/-0), sale_order.py (+32/-0)

**Message:**
> feat(v196): lock SO on invoice + lock invoice on post
>
> Two more locks in the Fields-disable branch — same pattern as
> v192 (ticket) / v193 (task) / v194 (picking) / v195 (xpath
> scope fix), applied to sale.order and account.move:
>   sale.order — freeze on invoice_count > 0
>   ------------------------------------------
>   Scope: Repair SOs only. Non-repair (Sales / Project) SOs
>   keep Odoo core's own readonly rules and Studio's gates
>   unchanged. Once an invoice exists, editing the SO would
>   drift the source document from the ledger — salesperson
>   must cancel/reverse the invoice first (dropping
>   invoice_count to 0) to reopen edits.
>   Gate:
>       x_studio_quotation_type == 'Repair'
>       and invoice_count > 0
>   account.move — freeze on state='posted'
>   ------------------------------------------
>   Scope: every invoice/move. Odoo core respects posted for
>   most fields natively, but Studio-added x_studio_* fields
>   and other view additions don't always honour it. Explicit
>   readonly closes that gap.
>   Gate:
>       state == 'posted'
> Both use the v195-corrected xpath predicate
>     //sheet//field[not(ancestor::field)]
> so embedded one2many views (order_line, invoice_line_ids,
> tax_totals, chatter) don't accidentally get the parent's lock
> expression stamped on their sub-fields.
> Preserves each field's existing readonly via
>     (existing) or (<gate>)
> so per-field readonly rules stay live before the record-level
> freeze kicks in.
> Version 17.0.1.0.195 → 17.0.1.0.196.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 61 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)
