> fix: merge duplicate write() overrides — restore serial -> product autofill
>
> v76 (3b04190) added a second def write(self, vals): on HelpdeskTicket
> without removing the original. Python silently kept only the second
> method, so the pre-existing serial -> product/SO re-assertion (added
> in 2427d38) stopped running.
> Merge both behaviours into a single write override:
>   1. Repair-Completed regression guard (pre-super, splits the recordset
>      by has_been_at_stage)
>   2. Extract the serial sync into _post_write_serial_product_sync and
>      call it after every super().write — covering both the split-write
>      paths and the normal pass-through path.
> After this commit there's exactly one write() method on the class and
> the serial autofill works again.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 35 insertions(+), 29 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.77] — 2026-06-25 — `e48c11f3`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+17/-8), stock_return_picking.py (+6/-0)

**Message:**
> fix: suppress ticket stage changes during dispatch wizard
>
> Add a context flag fix_repair_skip_ticket_stage_change=True at the
> start of stock.return.picking._create_returns. helpdesk.ticket.write
> now drops stage_id from any write whose env carries that flag.
> Effect: anything cascaded by dispatch picking creation — Studio
> automations, Odoo computes, indirect Path A/C/B fires — cannot change
> the ticket stage. Stage remains exactly where the user left it
> (Received at Sales Centre for Factory Repair, Repair Completed for
> Centre Repair). Once the user validates the dispatch picking
> explicitly, the validate flow runs without our wizard context, so
> Path B's existing 'Handed Over to Customer' transition still fires
> as expected.
> Keeps the prior Repair-Completed regression guard as a second line.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 24 insertions(+), 9 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.76] — 2026-06-25 — `3b041903`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+35/-0)

**Message:**
> fix: gate stage_id=Repair Completed at helpdesk.ticket.write() boundary
>
> Previous guard (in _move_to_stage and _move_ticket_to_stage) only
> caught calls through those two helpers. Some path is bypassing both —
> likely a Studio server action or an automation chain — and writing
> stage_id directly via .write().
> Override helpdesk.ticket.write to inspect every write call: if vals
> contains stage_id pointing at the 'Repair Completed' stage AND any
> ticket in self has already been at that stage, strip stage_id from
> this write for those tickets. Other tickets in the same recordset
> (first-time arrivers) and other vals are preserved.
> Works regardless of caller — Studio, computed cascade, our own code,
> external integration — because every stage change ultimately calls
> write.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 36 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.75] — 2026-06-25 — `b9abe176`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+26/-1), sale_order.py (+9/-1)

**Message:**
> fix: Repair Completed is a one-way milestone — never regress to it
>
> Symptom: after the ticket reaches Received at Sales Centre, creating
> the Dispatch return picking somehow flips the stage back to Repair
> Completed (root cause traced to upstream Studio chain, not isolated
> to a single code path).
> Defensive guard: every code path in our module that sets stage to
> 'Repair Completed' is centralised through two methods —
>   - helpdesk_ticket._move_to_stage (called by action_fsm_validate,
>     stock_picking._action_done Path B)
>   - sale_order._move_ticket_to_stage (called by stock_picking._action_done
>     Paths A and C)
> Both now consult ticket._has_been_at_stage('Repair Completed'), which
> queries mail.tracking.value for any prior write of stage_id =
> 'Repair Completed' on this ticket. If found, the move is silently
> skipped. First-time moves still pass through; only re-entries are
> blocked.
> Other stages remain unaffected — this gate is specific to Repair
> Completed, the milestone the user flagged.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 36 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.74] — 2026-06-25 — `cdc4711f`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+4/-1), stock_return_picking.py (+0/-1)

**Message:**
> fix: drop quantity=1.0 on stock.move create — was duplicating move_line
>
> In Odoo 17 stock.move.quantity is computed from sum(move_line_ids.quantity).
> Writing quantity=1.0 during create auto-materializes a move_line, then
> our explicit move_line create (carrying lot_id/qty_done for the
> serial) piled on a second ML — total quantity computed to 2 on every
> intransit / Plan-Intervention / Mark-as-Done picking.
> Drop quantity from the move create. The explicit move_line stays as
> the single source of truth; move.quantity computes to 1 from it.
> Applies to both _create_repair_transfer and the synthetic phantom in
> stock.return.picking.default_get.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 5 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)

> dropdown for the currently active company. Domain limits choices to

## [17.0.1.0.49] — 2026-06-24 — `c4f7f8e5`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: stock_warehouse.py (+38/-1)
- **Data records (XML)**: fix_repair_data.xml (+6/-0)

**Message:**
> feat: auto-seed factory_repair_location ir.config_parameter on upgrade
>
> Drops the manual System-Parameters step. data/fix_repair_data.xml now
> calls stock.warehouse._seed_factory_repair_locations() on every install
> and upgrade. The helper:
>   - Iterates res.company
>   - Matches each company name against FACTORY_LOCATION_DEFAULTS
>     (currently 'Jinasena Agricultural Machinery' -> 'PW-JM')
>   - Resolves to that warehouse's lot_stock_id
>   - Writes ir.config_parameter
>     'fix_repair.factory_repair_location.<company_id>' = <loc_id>
> Idempotent: never overwrites an existing parameter, so manual overrides
> survive future upgrades. Extending coverage to other companies is a
> matter of adding rows to FACTORY_LOCATION_DEFAULTS — no DB migration.

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 45 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

**Message:**
> fix: drop new DB columns; store factory location in ir.config_parameter
>
> Previous push added a DB column (res_company.factory_repair_location_id
> and stock_warehouse.intransit_warehouse_id) without a corresponding
> module update on Odoo SH, leaving the staging instance dead with
> 'column does not exist' SELECT errors on every page load.
> Roll back to a no-migration design:
> - res_company.factory_repair_location_id removed; per-company factory
>   location now lives in ir.config_parameter under key
>   fix_repair.factory_repair_location.<company_id> (value = location id
>   as string). helpdesk_ticket._get_factory_repair_location reads it.
>   Set it via Settings -> Technical -> System Parameters.
> - stock_warehouse.intransit_warehouse_id removed. _ensure_intransit_warehouse
>   now resolves pairing by code only: tries IT-/IW-/IB-<suffix> in turn,
>   reuses any match (auto-pairs to the existing IW-JM / IB-EM
>   warehouses), creates IT-<suffix> otherwise.
> After SH restarts on this push every page should load again. To set
> the factory location:
>   Settings -> Technical -> System Parameters -> New
>     key:   fix_repair.factory_repair_location.2
>     value: <id of PW-JM/Stock>

**Message:**
> refactor: ticket-owned movements via smart button, drop SO bind
>
> Previous flow re-pointed every ticket picking onto the repair SO
> (origin / group_id / sale_line_id on moves) by adding a price-0
> placeholder line. That spilled artifacts onto the SO: 1-N phantom
> deliveries plus a placeholder line showing qty_delivered: 4.
> Cleaner ownership: the helpdesk ticket already owns the repair
> lifecycle, and every ticket picking is stamped with
> x_studio_helpdesk_ticket_id when created. Surface them directly on
> the ticket:
> - helpdesk.ticket.repair_picking_ids (One2many on the existing Studio
>   field) + repair_picking_count + action_view_repair_pickings.
> - Movements smart button on the ticket form (truck icon, hidden when
>   count is 0).
> - project_task._bind_ticket_pickings_to_so and the placeholder-line
>   creation are gone. _fsm_create_sale_order is back to its plain
>   delegate.
> The repair SO now contains only real billable lines (parts/labour);
> the ticket is the one-stop view for everything that physically moved.

**Stats:** 4 files changed, 40 insertions(+), 89 deletions(-)

>

**Message:**
> feat: bind return pickings to the repair SO once it exists, not the original
>
> For every ticket type, the return wizard now synthesises a "Delivery to
> Return" without looking up the original sale order — the repair flow
> doesn't care which sale put the item in the customer's hands. The
> synthetic picking and every later collection picking are stamped with
> x_studio_helpdesk_ticket_id.
> When the FSM task creates its sale order, _bind_ticket_pickings_to_so
> runs: it adds a placeholder line for the ticket product, ensures a
> procurement group on the SO, and writes origin / group_id / sale_line_id
> on every ticket-tagged picking so picking.sale_id resolves to the repair
> SO. The SO's Delivery smart button then counts every movement from the
> ticket's lifecycle.

>

> has started, and the rest of the workflow handles payment reconciliation.

> 'other').

- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+7/-1)

**Message:**
> feat: drop x_studio_serial_no required gate for Without-Serial-No tickets
>
> Mirror the Assign-to-Me trick: just as ticket_type_id isn't required
> until a user is assigned (so Assign to Me is clickable on a brand-new
> ticket), x_studio_serial_no should not be required when the ticket
> type is Without Serial No. That flow generates the serial via the
> Create Serial No button — making the field required upfront blocks
> the save that the button click triggers, so the button could never
> fire on a fresh ticket.
> Required now means: ticket_type_id is set AND the ticket is NOT a
> Without-Serial-No type. RUG / With-Serial-No types still require the
> serial to be populated up front (it's picked manually there, not
> generated).

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 8 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.21] — 2026-06-18 — `4f1ae771`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+21/-1)

**Message:**
> fix: rewire Reject RUG to our method so line prices aren't zeroed
>
> The Studio server action 2004 (Reject RUG button) writes price_unit
> from x_studio_price_unit_original on every order line. That 'original'
> is only captured by Studio action 2144 when x_studio_rug_confirmed flips
> True. In a request → reject cycle where rug_confirmed never flips, the
> 'original' stays at 0 and the reset wipes the real sales price the
> quote already had.
> Rewire button name=2004 to action_reject_rug_direct on sale.order,
> which just writes {x_studio_rug_rejected: True} and leaves each
> line's price_unit alone.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 22 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

---

- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+0/-17)

**Message:**
> revert: drop Reject-RUG repricing — lines already carry correct sales price
>
> Reverts the previous commit's reject-RUG repricing. Pre-RUG-rejection
> the SO lines already hold the proper sales price from the quotation, so
> recomputing them at the moment of rejection only risks overwriting a
> correct value. Approve-RUG repricing to cost remains unchanged.

## [17.0.1.0.19] — 2026-06-18 — `3b05e768`

**Changed:**
- **Metadata**: __manifest__.py (version bump) (+1/-1)
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+17/-0)

**Message:**
> feat: on Reject RUG, reprice SO lines from the sales pricelist
>
> Mirror the Accept-RUG repricing (lines → product cost) with a
> Reject-RUG path: when x_studio_rug_rejected flips True, recompute
> every product line's price_unit from the SO's sales pricelist. The
> warranty had set every line to 0; now that the customer pays we
> need a real price on each line so an invoice can be raised against
> the SO with qty 1 and the configured sales price.
> Uses sale.order.line._compute_price_unit() which derives the price
> from order_id.pricelist_id (the sales pricelist on the SO), so
> customer-specific pricelists are respected.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 18 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

**Changed:**

**Message:**
> feat: route Reject-RUG repairs through the NUW (customer-pays) flow end-to-end
>

> repair — so invoicing and delivery should behave like Not Under Warranty,
> not like a still-under-warranty repair. Extend the existing NUW handling
> to also fire for x_studio_rug_rejected = True:

>     "wait until ticket reaches Repair Completed" gate when the RUG was
>     rejected (matches the NUW flow where the invoice can be raised
>     immediately).
>   • stock.picking nuw_block_validate — also blocks delivery validation
>     on Reject-RUG pickings until the ticket hits Advance Received /
>     Repair Started (customer must pay first).
>   • stock.picking _action_done — Reject-RUG SOs now move through
>     Path C (NUW stage routing) instead of Path A (warranty routing).

**Stats:** 3 files changed, 38 insertions(+), 13 deletions(-)

>

> force a module upgrade.

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `b0d2e0e2`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: project_task.py (+25/-10)

**Message:**
> fix: sync quotation_type whenever task-SO link is established
>
> Previous code only set x_studio_quotation_type when _fsm_ensure_sale_order
> created a brand-new SO (guarded by 'if not self.sale_order_id'). If the SO
> already existed on the task, or was linked later via write(), the type was
> never updated — so External not RUG tickets stayed as 'Repair', hiding the
> Send by Email button.
> Extract _sync_quotation_type() and call it:
>   - at the end of _fsm_ensure_sale_order (covers new + pre-existing SO)
>   - in write() when sale_order_id changes (covers manual SO linking)
> Rule: x_studio_rug_repair=True → 'Repair'; everything else → 'Not Under Warranty'

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 25 insertions(+), 10 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `42e117c3`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+81/-93)

**Message:**
> feat: Without Serial No — auto-create fake done delivery for return wizard
>
> Rather than running _action_done() (which triggers serial quant constraints),
> create the 'Delivery to Return' by writing state='done' directly on picking,
> move, and move_line records. No quants are touched → serial constraint never
> fires.
> Flow:
>   default_get creates fake done picking (Repair Loc → Customer) with the
>   ticket's serial, sets it as picking_id → wizard opens with delivery
>   pre-selected and readonly (user cannot swap it).
>   _create_returns (super) reverses the fake picking → new incoming picking
>   (Customer → Repair Loc). Post-creation: lot overridden to ticket serial,
>   move_lines created if absent so user just clicks Validate.
>   _get_view makes picking_id readonly for Without Serial No and hides
>   sale_order_id.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 81 insertions(+), 93 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `2cccc658`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+93/-73)

**Message:**
> feat: Without Serial No — wizard-based collection with no fake outgoing picking
>
> Instead of creating a fake outgoing picking (which triggers serial constraints),
> the wizard is now populated directly from the ticket:
> - default_get: leaves picking_id empty for Without Serial No tickets
> - _compute_moves_locations: when picking_id is empty and ticket is Without
>   Serial No, manually creates one product_return_moves line from the ticket
>   product and sets location_id to the repair location
> - _create_returns: for the Without Serial No / no-picking case, creates the
>   incoming collection picking directly (Customer → Repair Loc) with the serial
>   pre-filled, then returns its id so the wizard navigates to it for validation
> - _get_view: hides picking_id and sale_order_id fields for this flow
> A fresh serial (created via "Create Serial No") has no quants anywhere, so
> creating the collection picking produces exactly one positive quant (at
> repair_loc) — the serial constraint never fires.
> Return button on helpdesk ticket reverted to action 195 for all ticket types.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 93 insertions(+), 73 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `e6640089`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+6/-6)

**Message:**
> fix: use company-scoped inventory location for Without Serial No picking
>
> env.ref('stock.location_inventory') returns the company-1 virtual location.
> In a multi-company setup this causes "Incompatible companies" when creating
> a picking for company 2.
> Replace with a search that prefers a company-specific inventory location for
> the ticket's company, falling back to a global (company_id=False) location.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 6 insertions(+), 6 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `9eacde76`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+2/-68), stock_return_picking.py (+112/-43)

**Message:**
> fix: use inventory location for Without Serial No fake outgoing picking
>
> Previous approach used repair_loc as source → _action_done() briefly had two
> positive serial quants (repair_loc +1, cust_loc +1), triggering Odoo 17's
> stock.quant._check_serial_number constraint ("already assigned").
> Root cause: the constraint search excludes usage='inventory' locations. By
> using the standard inventory adjustment location as the source instead:
>   inventory_loc (+1) → cust_loc (+1) during _action_done
>   inventory_loc is excluded from the constraint check → no conflict.
> Flow for Without Serial No:
>   1. _update_available_quantity puts serial into inventory_loc
>   2. Outgoing picking inventory_loc → cust_loc is created and validated
>   3. Return wizard opens with picking pre-filled, sale_order_id hidden
>   4. _compute_moves_locations overrides location_id → repair_loc (suggested)
>   5. _create_returns creates collection picking: cust_loc → repair_loc
> Also removes action_collect_without_serial (no longer needed) and restores
> action 195 Return button for all ticket types.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 114 insertions(+), 111 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `d8e67578`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+68/-2), stock_return_picking.py (+37/-98)

**Message:**
> fix: bypass return wizard for Without Serial No — direct collection picking
>
> Odoo 17's stock.quant._check_serial_number constraint fires when a serial
> has positive quantity in two non-inventory locations simultaneously. This
> happens during _action_done() on any outgoing picking (source quant and
> destination quant both positive briefly) — it cannot be suppressed.
> The fake "Delivery to Return" approach is incompatible with serial-tracked
> products. For Without Serial No tickets, bypass the return wizard entirely:
> - Return button (action 195) is now hidden for x_studio_normal_repair_without_serial_no
> - New "Return" button calls action_collect_without_serial (Python method)
>   which directly creates an incoming picking (Customer → Repair Loc) with
>   the ticket's serial pre-filled, then opens it for the user to validate
> - Dispatch button (action 195) still appears for all types once
>   has_return_picking is True (incoming move from customer location detected)
> stock_return_picking.py reverted to With Serial No branch only.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 105 insertions(+), 100 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-10 — `4e582fea`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+6/-0)

**Message:**
> fix: create quant before outgoing picking in Without Serial No return flow
>
> The serial number created by action_create_repair_serial has no quant —
> no stock exists in any location. Calling _action_done() on the outgoing
> picking without a quant in the source location caused Odoo's serial
> constraint to fire: "The serial number has already been assigned".
> Fix: call stock.quant._update_available_quantity() to place 1 unit of
> the serial into the repair location before creating the outgoing picking.
> The picking then draws from that quant cleanly, and the return wizard can
> reverse it into the collection picking without constraint errors.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 6 insertions(+)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-09 — `cf3c83bb`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+21/-2), stock_return_picking.py (+95/-50)

**Message:**
> feat: Without Serial No — Return button creates collection picking via wizard
>
> For tickets with x_studio_normal_repair_without_serial_no=True, no historical
> outgoing delivery exists to reverse. When the Return button (action 195) opens
> the stock.return.picking wizard, default_get now:
>   1. Creates a new outgoing picking (Repair Loc → Customer, validated) as a
>      "Delivery to Return" — purely a technical artifact to satisfy the wizard.
>   2. Sets it as picking_id so the wizard can reverse it into the collection
>      picking (incoming: Customer → Repair Loc).
>   3. Does not set sale_order_id (no SO exists for this flow).
> _get_view hides sale_order_id in the wizard for Without Serial No tickets.
> _compute_has_return_picking now falls back to checking stock.move.line for an
> incoming move from a customer location when picking_ids is empty (no SO link),
> so the Return button correctly switches to Dispatch after the item is collected.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 116 insertions(+), 52 deletions(-)

>

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-07 — `ef24cc8b`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+14/-9)

**Message:**
> fix: deactivate only the clearing automation (243), not the populating one (172)
>
> Automation 243 ('RR - Auto Select Product for RUG Repairs-33') fires on
> x_studio_serial_no (on_change) and unconditionally clears product_id,
> lot_id, sale_order_id — even when a valid serial is selected. This is
> why selecting a serial number did not auto-populate product.
> Automation 172 ('RR - Auto Select Product for RUG Repairs') fires on
> ticket_type_id and correctly sets product from the serial — this is
> why changing ticket type (with serial already set) worked correctly.
> Previous deactivation code used an elif name fragment match that would
> have caught both automations. Now only targets automations whose
> on_change_field_ids includes x_studio_serial_no (field 26809), which
> selects automation 243 only.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 14 insertions(+), 9 deletions(-)

---

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-07 — `2e166ab9`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+37/-9)

**Message:**
> fix: reliably auto-populate product from serial number on helpdesk ticket
>
> Two-layer defence against Studio automations that clear product_id:
> 1. _deactivate_clearing_serial_automation: now searches by on_change
>    field trigger (x_studio_serial_no field ID) instead of exact name,
>    so renamed copies of the automation are also caught.
> 2. write() override: re-asserts product_id AFTER super().write()
>    completes. Studio on_create_or_write automations fire inside
>    super(), so any clearing they do is overwritten here. Context flag
>    _syncing_serial_product prevents infinite recursion.
> The @api.onchange keeps the real-time UI update working.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 37 insertions(+), 9 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-05 — `cd937b9a`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+11/-19)

**Message:**
> fix: override _compute_moves_locations to set Return Location correctly
>
> Any write to location_id outside the compute causes Odoo to re-run
> _compute_moves_locations to sync the co-computed fields (product_return_moves,
> original_location_id, etc.), which overwrites the change.  The ONLY
> reliable place to control location_id is inside the compute itself.
> Restore the compute override approach: call super() to populate all
> fields (including original_location_id), then set
> location_id = original_location_id so Return Location matches the
> Suggested Return Location shown in the wizard.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 11 insertions(+), 19 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-05 — `84634637`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+15/-4)

**Message:**
> fix: override create() to set Return Location = Suggested Return Location
>
> @api.onchange only fires on user-triggered field changes, so it misses
> the case where picking_id is pre-set from context when the wizard opens.
> Override create() to write location_id = original_location_id after
> super() has run all stored-field computes.  Keep @api.onchange as well
> to handle the case where the user changes the Delivery to Return field
> or picking cascades from a sale_order_id selection.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 15 insertions(+), 4 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-05 — `56945a70`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+8/-10)

**Message:**
> fix: use onchange to sync Return Location from Suggested Return Location
>
> Replace the compute override (which lost the race against the stored-field
> compute) with @api.onchange('picking_id').  Onchanges fire after computes
> in the wizard RPC cycle, so this reliably sets location_id to
> picking_id.location_id (the picking's source location = Suggested Return
> Location) regardless of the picking type's default_location_return_id.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 8 insertions(+), 10 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-06-05 — `38544270`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_return_picking.py (+12/-1)

**Message:**
> fix: auto-set Return Location to Suggested Return Location
>
> Override _compute_moves_locations to set location_id = original_location_id
> (the picking's source location) after the parent compute runs.
> The parent defaults location_id to the picking type's
> default_location_return_id, which diverges from original_location_id.
> Setting them equal means the Return Location field is pre-filled with
> the Suggested Return Location value as soon as a picking is selected.
> Works for both flows:
>   New stage (outgoing delivery): original = warehouse stock → return to warehouse
>   Received at Sales Centre (incoming return): original = customer → return to customer

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 12 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

> selection_add only works when the base field is defined in Python.
> x_studio_quotation_type is a Studio field (defined only in ir.model.fields
> in the DB, no Python class definition), so Odoo's ORM had no base to
> extend and raised an error at class-definition time.
> Remove the Python field definition entirely. The _get_view injection for
> the Send by Email button is unaffected. The 'Not Under Warranty' selection
> value must be added via Odoo Studio instead.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 1 insertion(+), 6 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-28 — `86fbbdb5`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: stock_picking.py (+0/-2)

**Message:**
> fix: Path B now moves ticket to Handed Over regardless of Repair SO
>
> The 2nd return picking (Virtual Repair → Customer) inherits the Repair
> SO's sale_id, which caused Path B to skip it (deferring to Path A).
> Path A only finds the ticket via a linked FSM task; when no task exists
> it silently does nothing, leaving the ticket stuck at Received at Sales
> Centre.
> Removing the Repair SO exclusion makes Path B a universal handler for
> inventory→customer handovers. If Path A also fires, _move_to_stage is
> idempotent so there is no double-move side effect.

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+27/-9)
- **View**: helpdesk_ticket_views.xml (+8/-9)

**Message:**
> Fix KeyError 27: stored computed field avoids many2one traversal in web_read
>
> stage_id.name in invisible conditions forces web_read to traverse
> helpdesk.stage as a many2one. Stage id=27 is filtered by record rules
> in certain user contexts, causing KeyError: 27.
> Fix: repair_stage_state is now store=True with @api.depends('stage_id').
> web_read reads the plain varchar from the DB column — no many2one lookup,
> no record-rule filtering. Button conditions use repair_stage_state values.

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-25 — `4c2cfffd`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: helpdesk_ticket.py (+2/-29)
- **View**: helpdesk_ticket_views.xml (+4/-9)

**Message:**
> Fix KeyError 27: drop repair_stage_state computed field
>
> The @api.depends('stage_id.name') dotted path caused web_read to include
> stage_id in the many2one fetch. Stage id=27 (Received at Sales Centre,
> team 3) was filtered by record rules in certain user contexts, missing
> from many2one_data and crashing with KeyError: 27.
> Replace repair_stage_state with stage_id.name dot-notation directly in
> invisible conditions — Odoo 17 supports related field access in expressions.
> Removes the computed field and its problematic dependency entirely.

**Stats:** 2 files changed, 6 insertions(+), 38 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-20 — `c7243ef7`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+10/-0)

**Message:**
> feat: auto-set Quotation Type to Repair when SO created from repair task
>
> When a sale order is created with a task_id and that task has a linked
> helpdesk ticket, x_studio_quotation_type defaults to 'Repair'. This
> covers the case where the field is immediately readonly (task_id != False)
> so the user cannot set it manually.

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-20 — `16eced2d`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+4/-6)

**Message:**
> fix: use sudo() throughout _move_ticket_to_stage to bypass company record rules
>
> Task/ticket lookups were silently returning empty when the user's session
> company context didn't match the SO's company, causing the stage move to
> be skipped without error.

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-20 — `e12d5d70`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+7/-4)

**Message:**
> Use allowed_company_ids context instead of hardcoded Studio field
>
> Replace the hardcoded x_studio_company_id domain filter with
> with_context(allowed_company_ids=[order.company_id.id]) so Odoo's own
> multi-company record rule filters the stage to the correct company.
> No Studio field names in the domain — works regardless of how the
> multi-company stage rule is implemented.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 7 insertions(+), 4 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-20 — `3122e0ee`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+3/-2)

**Message:**
> Filter stage by company to avoid cross-company stage selection
>
> Team filter alone returned stages from both companies when the same
> team spans multiple companies. Adding x_studio_company_id filter ensures
> we always pick the stage belonging to the sale order's company.

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 3 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-20 — `06fd54a3`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+3/-3)

**Message:**
> Use sudo() for helpdesk stage lookup and ticket write on RUG request
>
> Button-clicking users (e.g. sales reps) may lack read access to

> system action triggered by an authorized button click.

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+13/-10)

**Message:**
> Filter stage search by ticket team to fix multi-company access error
>
> Two stages named 'Estimation Sent to Customer' exist for different
> companies. Searching without a team filter returned the wrong company's
> stage (id=10), causing an access error for users in company 2.
> Now searches within the ticket's own team to get the correct stage.

## [17.0.1.0.0] — 2026-05-18 — `385e298e`

**Changed:**
- **Model/field**: sale_order.py (+40/-2)

**Message:**
> RUG approval: restrict buttons to Repair quotations, reprice on approve
>
> - REQUEST RUG APPROVAL (1980): only visible for Repair quotations in
>   draft/sent state, before approval request is sent
> - APPROVE/REJECT RUG (1981/2004): only visible for Repair quotations in
>   draft/sent state, after request is sent
> - On RUG approval: all order line prices are set to product standard_price
>   (cost price) so the order is accounted as an internal warranty cost
> - On rejection: prices remain unchanged (treated as a normal customer sale)

**Stats:** 1 file changed, 40 insertions(+), 2 deletions(-)

- **Model/field**: +sale_order.py (+14/-0)
- **View**: -sale_order_views.xml (+0/-26)
- **Module wiring**: models/__init__.py (+1/-0)

**Message:**
> Fix Order Payment Type readonly in Quotation state
>
> Replace the broken XML xpath approach (which failed because
> x_studio_order_payment_method lives in the Studio child view, not
> sale.view_order_form) with a Python _get_view() override that patches
> the compiled arch at request time.
> The Studio readonly condition locks the field for Cash customers even
> in draft/sent state. The override replaces it with a simple state-based
> rule: editable until the order is confirmed.
